4. Run the free sample with Node.js 20 or newer.

Save https://767-2676.com/schedule/sample.mjs, read it, then run:
node sample.mjs

Shortcut, if you accept running fetched code directly:
curl -fsS https://767-2676.com/schedule/sample.mjs | node --input-type=module
On Windows PowerShell, use curl.exe in place of curl.

The sample runs offline; no wallet, API key, or runtime dependencies.
It contains these same bytes, receipt, and historical public key, and uses the
maintained verifier. Expected: verified=true, digest_matches=true,
one_byte_who_tamper_rejected=true.
Window at the checkpoint, not now: [2026-09-07T07:59:50.252Z, 2026-09-07T08:00:10.252Z].
This is a real saved receipt. It is historical evidence, not current time.
Bundled keys reproduce the sample; obtain trusted issuer keys independently for real tasks.

5. Get fresh time for your own decision:
GET https://767-2676.com/v1/time
Without payment, expect HTTP 402 with x402 terms. A fresh signed time receipt
costs $0.001 USDC on Base. It does not require an API key; payment requires an
authorized wallet. The verifier derives a current interval using the signed
fields and measured monotonic durations. Do not reuse this sample as now.

Where the work stands
https://767-2676.com/where-the-work-stands
True Witness Here



  
Where the work stands

  
The mirror. It shows, never scores, and never chooses.


  

    
    

  

  
Drag to look around. Where the work stands does not change.


  


  

    

      
Needs

      
    
        
  • A first merchant willing to integrate
    • 
        
  • People with agents willing to try it
    • 
        
  • The means to defend it
    • 
      

    

    

      
Commitments

      
    
        
  • The mechanism is built and working
    • 
        
  • The filings are filed
    • 
        
  • The paper trail exists
    • 
        
  • She keeps showing up
    • 
      

    

    

      
Boundaries

      
    
        
  • Not giving it away
    • 
        
  • No shipping on asleep approval
    • 
        
  • Evidence, never permission
    • 
        
  • The work speaks, not the budget
    • 
      

    

  


  
The mirror shows. It never scores. It never chooses.

schemas/witness-request.v1.json
https://767-2676.com/schemas/witness-request.v1.json
{
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",
  "$id": "https://767-2676.com/schemas/witness-request.v1.json",
  "title": "POPCORN payload-bound witness request v1",
  "type": "object",
  "additionalProperties": false,
  "required": [
    "payload_digest",
    "nonce",
    "previous_attestation_digest"
  ],
  "properties": {
    "payload_digest": {
      "type": "object",
      "additionalProperties": false,
      "required": [
        "algorithm",
        "value"
      ],
      "properties": {
        "algorithm": {
          "type": "string",
          "const": "sha-256"
        },
        "value": {
          "type": "string",
          "pattern": "^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{43}$",
          "description": "Canonical unpadded base64url encoding of exactly 32 bytes."
        }
      }
    },
    "nonce": {
      "type": "string",
      "pattern": "^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{43}$",
      "description": "Canonical unpadded base64url encoding of 32 random bytes."
    },
    "previous_attestation_digest": {
      "description": "SHA-256 over the exact signed payload bytes of the predecessor compact JWS, obtained by base64url-decoding its second segment. The digest value uses the same canonical unpadded base64url encoding as payload_digest. Null starts a chain.",
      "anyOf": [
        {
          "type": "object",
          "additionalProperties": false,
          "required": [
            "algorithm",
            "value"
          ],
          "properties": {
            "algorithm": {
              "type": "string",
              "const": "sha-256"
            },
            "value": {
              "type": "string",
              "pattern": "^[A-Za-z0-9_-]{43}$",
              "description": "Canonical unpadded base64url encoding of exactly 32 bytes."
            }
          }
        },
        {
          "type": "null"
        }
      ]
    }
  }
}

{
  "$schema": "https://json-schema.org/draft/2020-12/schema",

    "witness_receipt",
